===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/components/LocatariosTable.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/components/LocatariosTable.tsx

import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import type { Locatario } from "../tipos";

type Props = { itens: Locatario[] };

export function LocatariosTable({ itens }: Props) {
  if (itens.length === 0) {
    return <div className="rounded-xl border bg-white p-4 text-sm text-slate-600">Nenhum locatário encontrado.</div>;
  }

  return (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-2xl border bg-white">
      <table className="min-w-full border-collapse text-sm">
        <thead>
          <tr className="border-b bg-slate-50 text-left">
            <th className="px-4 py-3">Nome</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3">Slug</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3">E-mail</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3">Status</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3">Ações</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {itens.map((item) => (
            <tr key={item.locatarioId} className="border-b last:border-b-0">
              <td className="px-4 py-3">{item.nome}</td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3">{item.slug}</td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3">{item.email}</td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3">{item.status}</td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
                  <Link className="text-blue-600 underline" to={`/plataforma/locatarios/${item.locatarioId}`}>Detalhes</Link>
                  <Link className="text-blue-600 underline" to={`/plataforma/locatarios/${item.locatarioId}/editar`}>Editar</Link>
                  <Link className="text-blue-600 underline" to={`/plataforma/locatarios/${item.locatarioId}/usuarios/novo`}>Novo usuário</Link>
                </div>
              </td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}

===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/components/UsuariosLocatarioTable.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/components/UsuariosLocatarioTable.tsx

import type { UsuarioLocatario } from "../api/usuariosLocatarioApi";

type Props = {
  itens: UsuarioLocatario[];
  onEditar?: (usuarioId: string) => void;
  onBloquear?: (usuarioId: string) => void;
  onReativar?: (usuarioId: string) => void;
  onResetarSenha?: (usuarioId: string) => void;
};

export function UsuariosLocatarioTable({
  itens,
  onEditar,
  onBloquear,
  onReativar,
  onResetarSenha,
}: Props) {
  if (itens.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-xl border bg-white p-4 text-sm text-slate-600">
        Nenhum usuário cadastrado para este locatário.
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-2xl border bg-white">
      <table className="min-w-full border-collapse text-sm">
        <thead>
          <tr className="border-b bg-slate-50 text-left">
            <th className="px-4 py-3">Nome</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3">E-mail</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3">Perfil</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3">Status</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3">ID</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3">Ações</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>

        <tbody>
          {itens.map((usuario) => {
            const status = usuario.status?.trim().toUpperCase();
            const ativo = status === "ATIVO";
            const bloqueado =
              status === "BLOQUEADO" ||
              status === "INATIVO" ||
              status === "SUSPENSO";

            return (
              <tr
                key={usuario.usuarioId}
                className="border-b last:border-b-0"
              >
                <td className="px-4 py-3">{usuario.nome}</td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3">{usuario.email}</td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3">{usuario.perfil ?? "—"}</td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3">{usuario.status ?? "—"}</td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3">{usuario.usuarioId}</td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3">
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
                    <button
                      className="text-blue-600 underline disabled:opacity-50"
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => onEditar?.(usuario.usuarioId)}
                    >
                      Editar
                    </button>

                    {ativo ? (
                      <button
                        className="text-red-600 underline disabled:opacity-50"
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => onBloquear?.(usuario.usuarioId)}
                      >
                        Bloquear
                      </button>
                    ) : null}

                    {bloqueado ? (
                      <button
                        className="text-green-700 underline disabled:opacity-50"
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => onReativar?.(usuario.usuarioId)}
                      >
                        Reativar
                      </button>
                    ) : null}

                    <button
                      className="text-blue-600 underline disabled:opacity-50"
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => onResetarSenha?.(usuario.usuarioId)}
                    >
                      Resetar senha
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </td>
              </tr>
            );
          })}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}

===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/hooks/useLocatarioActions.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/hooks/useLocatarioActions.ts

import { useState } from "react";
import { mapError } from "../../../../core/errors/mapError";
import { locatariosApi } from "../api/locatariosApi";

type ActionName = "suspender" | "reativar" | "encerrar";

export function useLocatarioActions(onSuccess?: () => void | Promise<void>) {
  const [isRunning, setIsRunning] = useState<ActionName | null>(null);
  const [erro, setErro] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function suspender(locatarioId: string, motivoSuspensao?: string) {
    setIsRunning("suspender");
    setErro(null);

    try {
      await locatariosApi.suspender(locatarioId, { motivoSuspensao });
      await onSuccess?.();
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      setErro(mapError(error).message);
      return false;
    } finally {
      setIsRunning(null);
    }
  }

  async function reativar(locatarioId: string, motivoReativacao?: string) {
    setIsRunning("reativar");
    setErro(null);

    try {
      await locatariosApi.reativar(locatarioId, { motivoReativacao });
      await onSuccess?.();
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      setErro(mapError(error).message);
      return false;
    } finally {
      setIsRunning(null);
    }
  }

  async function encerrar(locatarioId: string, motivoExclusao?: string) {
    setIsRunning("encerrar");
    setErro(null);

    try {
      await locatariosApi.encerrar(locatarioId, { motivoExclusao });
      await onSuccess?.();
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      setErro(mapError(error).message);
      return false;
    } finally {
      setIsRunning(null);
    }
  }

  return {
    erro,
    isRunning,
    suspender,
    reativar,
    encerrar,
  };
}

===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/hooks/useLocatarioConfiguracoesActions.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/hooks/useLocatarioConfiguracoesActions.ts

import { useState } from "react";
import { mapError } from "../../../../core/errors/mapError";
import {
  locatarioConfiguracoesApi,
  type AtualizarBrandingLocatarioInput,
} from "../api/locatarioConfiguracoesApi";

type RunningAction = "branding" | "vincular-modulo" | "desvincular-modulo";

type Params = {
  locatarioId?: string;
  onSuccess?: () => void | Promise<void>;
};

export function useLocatarioConfiguracoesActions({ locatarioId, onSuccess }: Params) {
  const [isRunning, setIsRunning] = useState<RunningAction | null>(null);
  const [erro, setErro] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [sucesso, setSucesso] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function finalizar(mensagem: string) {
    setSucesso(mensagem);
    await onSuccess?.();
  }

  async function atualizarBranding(input: AtualizarBrandingLocatarioInput) {
    if (!locatarioId?.trim()) {
      setErro("ID do locatário inválido.");
      return false;
    }

    setIsRunning("branding");
    setErro(null);
    setSucesso(null);

    try {
      await locatarioConfiguracoesApi.atualizarBranding(locatarioId, input);
      await finalizar("Branding atualizado com sucesso.");
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      setErro(mapError(error).message);
      return false;
    } finally {
      setIsRunning(null);
    }
  }

  async function vincularModulo(codigo: string) {
    if (!locatarioId?.trim()) {
      setErro("ID do locatário inválido.");
      return false;
    }

    const codigoNormalizado = codigo.trim().toUpperCase();
    if (!codigoNormalizado) {
      setErro("Informe o código do módulo.");
      return false;
    }

    setIsRunning("vincular-modulo");
    setErro(null);
    setSucesso(null);

    try {
      await locatarioConfiguracoesApi.vincularModulo(locatarioId, {
        codigo: codigoNormalizado,
      });
      await finalizar("Módulo vinculado com sucesso.");
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      setErro(mapError(error).message);
      return false;
    } finally {
      setIsRunning(null);
    }
  }

  async function desvincularModulo(codigo: string) {
    if (!locatarioId?.trim()) {
      setErro("ID do locatário inválido.");
      return false;
    }

    const codigoNormalizado = codigo.trim().toUpperCase();
    if (!codigoNormalizado) {
      setErro("Informe o código do módulo.");
      return false;
    }

    const confirmado = window.confirm(`Remover o módulo ${codigoNormalizado} deste locatário?`);
    if (!confirmado) return false;

    setIsRunning("desvincular-modulo");
    setErro(null);
    setSucesso(null);

    try {
      await locatarioConfiguracoesApi.desvincularModulo(locatarioId, codigoNormalizado);
      await finalizar("Módulo removido com sucesso.");
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      setErro(mapError(error).message);
      return false;
    } finally {
      setIsRunning(null);
    }
  }

  return { atualizarBranding, vincularModulo, desvincularModulo, isRunning, erro, sucesso };
}


===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/hooks/useLocatarioConfiguracoes.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/hooks/useLocatarioConfiguracoes.ts

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { mapError } from "../../../../core/errors/mapError";
import {
  locatarioConfiguracoesApi,
  type BrandingLocatario,
  type ModuloAssinaturaLocatario,
} from "../api/locatarioConfiguracoesApi";

export function useLocatarioConfiguracoes(locatarioId?: string) {
  const [branding, setBranding] = useState<BrandingLocatario | null>(null);
  const [modulos, setModulos] = useState<ModuloAssinaturaLocatario[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [erro, setErro] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const carregar = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!locatarioId?.trim()) {
      setErro("ID do locatário inválido.");
      setIsLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    setIsLoading(true);
    setErro(null);

    try {
      const [brandingResponse, modulosResponse] = await Promise.all([
        locatarioConfiguracoesApi.obterBranding(locatarioId),
        locatarioConfiguracoesApi.listarModulos(locatarioId),
      ]);

      setBranding(brandingResponse.item);
      setModulos(modulosResponse.modulos);
    } catch (error) {
      setErro(mapError(error).message);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }, [locatarioId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    void carregar();
  }, [carregar]);

  return { branding, modulos, isLoading, erro, recarregar: carregar };
}


===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/hooks/useLocatarios.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/hooks/useLocatarios.ts

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { mapError } from "../../../../core/errors/mapError";
import { locatariosApi } from "../api/locatariosApi";
import type { Locatario } from "../tipos";

export function useLocatarios() {
  const [itens, setItens] = useState<Locatario[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [erro, setErro] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const carregar = useCallback(async () => {
    setIsLoading(true);
    setErro(null);

    try {
      const response = await locatariosApi.listar();
      setItens(response.itens);
    } catch (error) {
      setErro(mapError(error).message);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    void carregar();
  }, [carregar]);

  return {
    itens,
    isLoading,
    erro,
    recarregar: carregar,
  };
}


===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/hooks/useLocatario.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/hooks/useLocatario.ts

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { mapError } from "../../../../core/errors/mapError";
import { locatariosApi } from "../api/locatariosApi";
import type { Locatario } from "../tipos";

export function useLocatario(locatarioId?: string) {
  const [item, setItem] = useState<Locatario | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [erro, setErro] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const carregar = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!locatarioId?.trim()) {
      setItem(null);
      setErro("ID do locatário inválido.");
      setIsLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    setIsLoading(true);
    setErro(null);

    try {
      const response = await locatariosApi.obter(locatarioId);
      setItem(response.item);
    } catch (error) {
      setErro(mapError(error).message);
      setItem(null);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }, [locatarioId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    void carregar();
  }, [carregar]);

  return {
    item,
    isLoading,
    erro,
    recarregar: carregar,
  };
}

===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/hooks/useSalvarLocatario.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/hooks/useSalvarLocatario.ts

import { useState } from "react";
import { mapError } from "../../../../core/errors/mapError";
import { locatariosApi } from "../api/locatariosApi";
import type { AtualizarLocatarioInput, CriarLocatarioInput } from "../tipos";

type Params = {
  mode: "create" | "edit";
  locatarioId?: string;
  onSuccess?: (locatarioId?: string) => void | Promise<void>;
};

export function useSalvarLocatario({ mode, locatarioId, onSuccess, }: Params) {
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);
  const [erro, setErro] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function salvar(input: CriarLocatarioInput | AtualizarLocatarioInput) {
    setIsSaving(true);
    setErro(null);

    try {
      if (mode === "create") {
        const response = await locatariosApi.criar(input as CriarLocatarioInput);
        await onSuccess?.(response.item.locatarioId);
      } else {
        const id = locatarioId?.trim();

        if (!id) {
          setErro("ID do locatário inválido.");
          return false;
        }

        await locatariosApi.atualizar(id, input as AtualizarLocatarioInput);
        await onSuccess?.(id);
      }

      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      setErro(mapError(error).message);
      return false;
    } finally {
      setIsSaving(false);
    }
  }

  return {
    salvar,
    isSaving,
    erro,
  };
}

      setErro("ID do locatário inválido.");
      setIsLoading(false);

    setErro(null);

      setIsLoading(false);
    }

    void carregar();

===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/pages/EditarLocatarioPage.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/pages/EditarLocatarioPage.tsx

import { useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { LocatarioBrandingForm } from "../components/LocatarioBrandingForm";
import { LocatarioForm } from "../components/LocatarioForm";
import { LocatarioModulosForm } from "../components/LocatarioModulosForm";
import { useLocatario } from "../hooks/useLocatario";
import { useLocatarioConfiguracoes } from "../hooks/useLocatarioConfiguracoes";
import { useLocatarioConfiguracoesActions } from "../hooks/useLocatarioConfiguracoesActions";
import { useSalvarLocatario } from "../hooks/useSalvarLocatario";
import type { AtualizarLocatarioInput } from "../tipos";

export default function EditarLocatarioPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const params = useParams<{ locatarioId: string }>();
  const locatarioId = params.locatarioId?.trim();

  const { item, isLoading, erro: erroCarga } = useLocatario(locatarioId);
  const { branding, modulos, isLoading: isLoadingConfiguracoes, erro: erroConfiguracoes, recarregar: recarregarConfiguracoes } = useLocatarioConfiguracoes(locatarioId);
  const { atualizarBranding, vincularModulo, desvincularModulo, isRunning, erro: erroAcaoConfiguracao, sucesso } = useLocatarioConfiguracoesActions({ locatarioId, onSuccess: recarregarConfiguracoes });

  const { salvar, isSaving, erro } = useSalvarLocatario({
    mode: "edit",
    locatarioId,
    onSuccess: (id) => navigate(id ? `/plataforma/locatarios/${id}` : "/plataforma/locatarios"),
  });

  async function handleSubmit(input: AtualizarLocatarioInput) {
    await salvar(input);
  }

  if (isLoading) return <div className="rounded-2xl border bg-white p-6">Carregando locatário...</div>;
  if (erroCarga) return <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">{erroCarga}</div>;
  if (!item) return <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">Locatário não encontrado.</div>;

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-6">
      <div><h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">Editar locatário</h1><p className="text-sm text-slate-600">Atualize cadastro, branding e módulos da assinatura.</p></div>

      <LocatarioForm mode="edit" initialData={item} isSaving={isSaving} erro={erro} onSubmit={handleSubmit} />

      {erroConfiguracoes ? <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">{erroConfiguracoes}</div> : null}
      {erroAcaoConfiguracao ? <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">{erroAcaoConfiguracao}</div> : null}
      {sucesso ? <div className="rounded-xl border border-green-200 bg-green-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-green-700">{sucesso}</div> : null}

      {isLoadingConfiguracoes ? (
        <div className="rounded-2xl border bg-white p-6">Carregando configurações...</div>
      ) : (
        <>
          <LocatarioBrandingForm branding={branding} isSaving={isRunning === "branding"} onSubmit={(input) => void atualizarBranding(input)} />
          <LocatarioModulosForm modulos={modulos} isRunning={isRunning !== null} onVincular={(codigo) => void vincularModulo(codigo)} onDesvincular={(codigo) => void desvincularModulo(codigo)} />
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/pages/LocatarioDetailsPage.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/pages/LocatarioDetailsPage.tsx

import { Link, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { LocatarioBrandingResumo } from "../components/LocatarioBrandingResumo";
import { LocatarioModulosResumo } from "../components/LocatarioModulosResumo";
import { useLocatario } from "../hooks/useLocatario";
import { useLocatarioActions } from "../hooks/useLocatarioActions";
import { useLocatarioConfiguracoes } from "../hooks/useLocatarioConfiguracoes";

function formatarData(value?: string | null): string {
  if (!value) return "—";
  return new Date(value).toLocaleString("pt-BR");
}

function normalizarStatus(status?: string | null): string {
  return status?.trim().toUpperCase() ?? "";
}

export default function LocatarioDetailsPage() {
  const params = useParams<{ locatarioId: string }>();
  const locatarioId = params.locatarioId?.trim();

  const { item, isLoading, erro, recarregar } = useLocatario(locatarioId);
  const {
    branding,
    modulos,
    isLoading: isLoadingConfiguracoes,
    erro: erroConfiguracoes,
    recarregar: recarregarConfiguracoes,
  } = useLocatarioConfiguracoes(locatarioId);

  const { erro: erroAcao, isRunning, suspender, reativar, encerrar } =
    useLocatarioActions(async () => {
      await recarregar();
      await recarregarConfiguracoes();
    });

  if (isLoading) {
    return <div className="rounded-2xl border bg-white p-6">Carregando locatário...</div>;
  }

  if (erro) {
    return <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">{erro}</div>;
  }

  if (!item || !locatarioId) {
    return <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">Locatário não encontrado.</div>;
  }

  const id = locatarioId;
  const status = normalizarStatus(item.status);
  const estaEncerrado = status === "ENCERRADO" || Boolean(item.excluidoEm);
  const estaSuspenso = !estaEncerrado && status.includes("SUSPENS");
  const estaAtivo = !estaEncerrado && status === "ATIVO";

  async function handleSuspender() {
    const motivoSuspensao = window.prompt("Motivo da suspensão do locatário:")?.trim() || undefined;
    await suspender(id, motivoSuspensao);
  }

  async function handleReativar() {
    const motivoReativacao = window.prompt("Motivo da reativação do locatário:")?.trim() || undefined;
    await reativar(id, motivoReativacao);
  }

  async function handleEncerrar() {
    const confirmado = window.confirm("Encerrar este locatário? Esta operação deve ser usada apenas quando o contrato/conta será finalizado.");
    if (!confirmado) return;
    const motivoExclusao = window.prompt("Motivo do encerramento do locatário:")?.trim() || undefined;
    await encerrar(id, motivoExclusao);
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-6">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-4">
        <div><h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">{item.nome}</h1><p className="text-sm text-slate-600">{item.email}</p></div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
          <Link className="rounded-xl border px-4 py-2" to={`/plataforma/locatarios/${id}/editar`}>Editar</Link>
          <Link className="rounded-xl border px-4 py-2" to={`/plataforma/locatarios/${id}/usuarios`}>Usuários</Link>
          {!estaEncerrado ? <Link className="rounded-xl border px-4 py-2" to={`/plataforma/locatarios/${id}/usuarios/novo`}>Novo usuário</Link> : null}
        </div>
      </div>

      {erroAcao ? <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">{erroAcao}</div> : null}

      <div className="grid gap-2 rounded-2xl border bg-white p-6 text-sm">
        <div><strong>ID:</strong> {item.locatarioId}</div><div><strong>Slug:</strong> {item.slug}</div><div><strong>Status:</strong> {item.status}</div>
        <div><strong>Documento:</strong> {item.documento ?? "—"}</div><div><strong>Fuso horário:</strong> {item.fusoHorario ?? "—"}</div>
        <div><strong>Criado em:</strong> {formatarData(item.criadoEm)}</div><div><strong>Atualizado em:</strong> {formatarData(item.atualizadoEm)}</div><div><strong>Excluído em:</strong> {formatarData(item.excluidoEm)}</div>
      </div>

      {erroConfiguracoes ? <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">{erroConfiguracoes}</div> : null}
      {isLoadingConfiguracoes ? <div className="rounded-2xl border bg-white p-6">Carregando configurações...</div> : <><LocatarioBrandingResumo branding={branding} /><LocatarioModulosResumo modulos={modulos} /></>}

      <div className="rounded-2xl border bg-white p-6">
        <h2 className="mb-3 text-lg font-semibold">Operações do locatário</h2>
        {estaAtivo ? <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3"><button className="rounded-xl border px-4 py-2" type="button" onClick={() => void handleSuspender()} disabled={isRunning !== null}>{isRunning === "suspender" ? "Suspendendo..." : "Suspender"}</button><button className="rounded-xl border px-4 py-2 text-red-700" type="button" onClick={() => void handleEncerrar()} disabled={isRunning !== null}>{isRunning === "encerrar" ? "Encerrando..." : "Encerrar"}</button></div> : null}
        {estaSuspenso ? <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3"><button className="rounded-xl border px-4 py-2" type="button" onClick={() => void handleReativar()} disabled={isRunning !== null}>{isRunning === "reativar" ? "Reativando..." : "Reativar"}</button><button className="rounded-xl border px-4 py-2 text-red-700" type="button" onClick={() => void handleEncerrar()} disabled={isRunning !== null}>{isRunning === "encerrar" ? "Encerrando..." : "Encerrar"}</button></div> : null}
        {estaEncerrado ? <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">Locatário encerrado. Nenhuma operação operacional disponível.</p> : null}
        {!estaAtivo && !estaSuspenso && !estaEncerrado ? <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">Nenhuma operação disponível para o status atual: {item.status}.</p> : null}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/pages/LocatariosListPage.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/pages/LocatariosListPage.tsx

import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { LocatariosTable } from "../components/LocatariosTable";
import { useLocatarios } from "../hooks/useLocatarios";

export default function LocatariosListPage() {
  const { itens, isLoading, erro, recarregar } = useLocatarios();

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">Locatários</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">Gerencie os locatários da plataforma.</p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-3">
          <button className="rounded-xl border px-4 py-2" type="button" onClick={() => void recarregar()}>Recarregar</button>
          <Link className="rounded-xl border px-4 py-2" to="/plataforma/locatarios/novo">Novo locatário</Link>
        </div>
      </div>
      {erro ? <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">{erro}</div> : null}
      {isLoading ? <div className="rounded-2xl border bg-white p-6">Carregando locatários...</div> : <LocatariosTable itens={itens} />}
    </div>
  );
}


===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/pages/NovoLocatarioPage.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/pages/NovoLocatarioPage.tsx

import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { LocatarioForm } from "../components/LocatarioForm";
import { useSalvarLocatario } from "../hooks/useSalvarLocatario";
import type { AtualizarLocatarioInput, CriarLocatarioInput } from "../tipos";

export default function NovoLocatarioPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { salvar, isSaving, erro } = useSalvarLocatario({
    mode: "create",
    onSuccess: (locatarioId) => navigate(locatarioId ? `/plataforma/locatarios/${locatarioId}` : "/plataforma/locatarios"),
  });
  async function handleSubmit(input: CriarLocatarioInput | AtualizarLocatarioInput) { await salvar(input as CriarLocatarioInput); }
  return <div className="grid gap-6"><div><h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">Novo locatário</h1><p className="text-sm text-slate-600">Cadastre um novo locatário da plataforma.</p></div><LocatarioForm mode="create" isSaving={isSaving} erro={erro} onSubmit={handleSubmit} /></div>;
}


===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/pages/NovoUsuarioLocatarioPage.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/pages/NovoUsuarioLocatarioPage.tsx

import { useState } from "react";
import { Link, useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { mapError } from "../../../../core/errors/mapError";
import { UsuarioForm } from "../../../tenant-admin/usuarios";
import type { AtualizarUsuarioInput, CriarUsuarioInput } from "../../../tenant-admin/usuarios";
import { usuariosLocatarioApi } from "../api/usuariosLocatarioApi";

export default function NovoUsuarioLocatarioPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const params = useParams<{ locatarioId: string }>();
  const locatarioId = params.locatarioId?.trim();
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);
  const [erro, setErro] = useState<string | null>(null);
  async function handleSubmit(input: CriarUsuarioInput | AtualizarUsuarioInput) {
    if (!locatarioId) { setErro("ID do locatário inválido."); return; }
    setIsSaving(true); setErro(null);
    try { await usuariosLocatarioApi.criar(locatarioId, input as CriarUsuarioInput); navigate(`/plataforma/locatarios/${locatarioId}`, { replace: true }); }
    catch (error) { setErro(mapError(error).message); }
    finally { setIsSaving(false); }
  }
  if (!locatarioId) return <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">ID do locatário inválido.</div>;
  return <div className="grid gap-6"><div><h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">Novo usuário do locatário</h1><p className="text-sm text-slate-600">Cadastre um usuário diretamente pela área da plataforma.</p></div><div><Link className="text-blue-600 underline" to={`/plataforma/locatarios/${locatarioId}`}>Voltar ao locatário</Link></div><UsuarioForm mode="create" isSaving={isSaving} erro={erro} onSubmit={handleSubmit} /></div>;
}


===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/pages/UsuariosLocatarioListPage.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/pages/UsuariosLocatarioListPage.tsx

import { Link, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { UsuariosLocatarioTable } from "../components/UsuariosLocatarioTable";
import { useUsuariosLocatario } from "../hooks/useUsuariosLocatario";

export default function UsuariosLocatarioListPage() {
  const params = useParams<{ locatarioId: string }>();
  const locatarioId = params.locatarioId?.trim();

  const { itens, isLoading, erro, recarregar } =
    useUsuariosLocatario(locatarioId);

  if (!locatarioId) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
        ID do locatário inválido.
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-6">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">Usuários do locatário</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">
            Consulte os usuários cadastrados para este locatário.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
          <button
            className="rounded-xl border px-4 py-2"
            type="button"
            onClick={() => void recarregar()}
          >
            Recarregar
          </button>

          <Link
            className="rounded-xl border px-4 py-2"
            to={`/plataforma/locatarios/${locatarioId}`}
          >
            Voltar ao locatário
          </Link>

          <Link
            className="rounded-xl border px-4 py-2"
            to={`/plataforma/locatarios/${locatarioId}/usuarios/novo`}
          >
            Novo usuário
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>

      {erro ? (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
          {erro}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="rounded-2xl border bg-white p-6">
          Carregando usuários...
        </div>
      ) : (
        <UsuariosLocatarioTable itens={itens} />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/tipos.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/tipos.ts

export type StatusLocatario =
  | "ATIVO"
  | "SUSPENSO"
  | "ENCERRADO"
  | "INATIVO";

export type ModoTema = "SISTEMA" | "CLARO" | "ESCURO";

export type Locatario = {
  locatarioId: string;
  nome: string;
  slug: string;
  email: string;
  documento?: string | null;
  fusoHorario?: string | null;
  status: StatusLocatario;
  motivoSuspensao?: string | null;
  motivoExclusao?: string | null;
  criadoEm?: string;
  atualizadoEm?: string;
  excluidoEm?: string | null;
};

export type ConfiguracaoLocatario = {
  configuracaoId: string;
  locatarioId: string;
  modoTema?: ModoTema;
  identidadeVisual: string;
  corPrimaria?: string | null;
  corSecundaria?: string | null;
  corTerciaria?: string | null;
  urlLogo?: string | null;
  ativa: boolean;
  criadoEm?: string;
  atualizadoEm?: string;
};

export type CriarLocatarioInput = {
  nome: string;
  slug: string;
  email: string;
  documento?: string | null;
  fusoHorario?: string;
  configuracaoInicial: {
    modoTema?: ModoTema;
    identidadeVisual: string;
    corPrimaria?: string | null;
    corSecundaria?: string | null;
    corTerciaria?: string | null;
    urlLogo?: string | null;
    ativa: boolean;
  };
};

export type AtualizarLocatarioInput = {
  nome?: string;
  email?: string;
  documento?: string | null;
  fusoHorario?: string;
};

export type SuspenderLocatarioInput = {
  motivoSuspensao?: string | null;
};

export type ReativarLocatarioInput = {
  motivoReativacao?: string | null;
};

export type EncerrarLocatarioInput = {
  motivoExclusao?: string | null;
};

export type ListarLocatariosResponse = {
  itens: Locatario[];
};

export type ObterLocatarioResponse = {
  item: Locatario;
};

export type CriarLocatarioResponse = {
  item: {
    locatarioId: string;
  };
};

export type AtualizarLocatarioResponse = {
  item: Locatario;
};

export type AcaoLocatarioResponse = {
  item: Locatario;
};

===== apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/assinatura/api/assinaturaModulosApi.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/assinatura/api/assinaturaModulosApi.ts

import { httpClient } from "../../../../core/api/httpClient";
import type { DesvincularModuloAssinaturaResponse, ListarMeusModulosAssinaturaResponse, VincularModuloAssinaturaInput, VincularModuloAssinaturaResponse } from "../tipos";

const BASE = "/me/assinatura/modulos";

export const assinaturaModulosApi = {
  listar(): Promise<ListarMeusModulosAssinaturaResponse> {
    return httpClient.get<ListarMeusModulosAssinaturaResponse>(BASE);
  },

  vincular(
    input: VincularModuloAssinaturaInput,
  ): Promise<VincularModuloAssinaturaResponse> {
    return httpClient.post<
      VincularModuloAssinaturaResponse,
      VincularModuloAssinaturaInput
    >(BASE, input);
  },

  desvincular(moduloId: string): Promise<DesvincularModuloAssinaturaResponse> {
    return httpClient.delete<DesvincularModuloAssinaturaResponse>(
      `${BASE}/${moduloId}`,
    );
  },
};

===== apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/assinatura/components/AssinaturaModulosTable.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/assinatura/components/AssinaturaModulosTable.tsx

import type { ModuloAssinatura } from "../tipos";

type Props = {
  modulos: ModuloAssinatura[];
  podeAtualizar?: boolean;
  isRunning?: boolean;
  onDesvincular?: (moduloCodigo: string) => void;
};

export function AssinaturaModulosTable({ modulos, podeAtualizar, isRunning, onDesvincular }: Props) {
  if (modulos.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-xl border bg-white p-4 text-sm text-slate-600">
        Nenhum módulo habilitado nesta assinatura.
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-2xl border bg-white">
      <table className="min-w-full border-collapse text-sm">
        <thead>
          <tr className="border-b bg-slate-50 text-left">
            <th className="px-4 py-3">Código</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3">Origem</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3">Ações</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>

        <tbody>
          {modulos.map((modulo) => (
            <tr key={modulo.codigo} className="border-b last:border-b-0">
              <td className="px-4 py-3">{modulo.codigo}</td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3">{modulo.origem}</td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3">
                {podeAtualizar ? (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="text-red-700 underline"
                    disabled={Boolean(isRunning)}
                    onClick={() => onDesvincular?.(modulo.codigo)}
                  >
                    Desvincular
                  </button>
                ) : (
                  <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                    Sem permissão de alteração
                  </span>
                )}
              </td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}

===== apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/assinatura/components/VincularModuloForm.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/assinatura/components/VincularModuloForm.tsx

import { useState } from "react";

type Props = {
  isSaving?: boolean;
  onSubmit: (moduloId: string) => void | Promise<void>;
};

export function VincularModuloForm({ isSaving, onSubmit }: Props) {
  const [moduloId, setModuloId] = useState("");

  async function handleSubmit(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();

    await onSubmit(moduloId);
    setModuloId("");
  }

  return (
    <form
      className="grid gap-4 rounded-2xl border bg-white p-6"
      onSubmit={handleSubmit}
    >
      <div>
        <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">Vincular módulo</h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">
          Informe o ID do módulo a ser habilitado na assinatura.
        </p>
      </div>

      <label className="grid gap-1">
        <span className="text-sm font-medium">ID do módulo</span>
        <input
          className="rounded-xl border px-3 py-2"
          value={moduloId}
          onChange={(event) => setModuloId(event.target.value)}
          required
        />
      </label>

      <div className="flex justify-end">
        <button
          className="rounded-xl border px-4 py-2 font-medium"
          type="submit"
          disabled={Boolean(isSaving)}
        >
          {isSaving ? "Vinculando..." : "Vincular"}
        </button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

===== apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/assinatura/hooks/useAssinaturaModuloActions.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/assinatura/hooks/useAssinaturaModuloActions.ts

import { useState } from "react";
import { mapError } from "../../../../core/errors/mapError";
import { assinaturaModulosApi } from "../api/assinaturaModulosApi";

type ActionName = "vincular" | "desvincular";

type Params = {
  onSuccess?: () => void | Promise<void>;
};

export function useAssinaturaModuloActions({ onSuccess }: Params = {}) {
  const [isRunning, setIsRunning] = useState<ActionName | null>(null);
  const [erro, setErro] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [sucesso, setSucesso] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function vincular(moduloId: string) {
    const id = moduloId.trim();

    if (!id) {
      setErro("Informe o ID do módulo.");
      return false;
    }

    setIsRunning("vincular");
    setErro(null);
    setSucesso(null);

    try {
      await assinaturaModulosApi.vincular({ moduloId: id });
      setSucesso("Módulo vinculado com sucesso.");
      await onSuccess?.();
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      setErro(mapError(error).message);
      return false;
    } finally {
      setIsRunning(null);
    }
  }

  async function desvincular(moduloId: string) {
    const id = moduloId.trim();

    if (!id) {
      setErro("Módulo inválido.");
      return false;
    }

    setIsRunning("desvincular");
    setErro(null);
    setSucesso(null);

    try {
      await assinaturaModulosApi.desvincular(id);
      setSucesso("Módulo desvinculado com sucesso.");
      await onSuccess?.();
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      setErro(mapError(error).message);
      return false;
    } finally {
      setIsRunning(null);
    }
  }

  return {
    vincular,
    desvincular,
    isRunning,
    erro,
    sucesso,
  };
}

===== apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/assinatura/hooks/useAssinaturaModulos.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/assinatura/hooks/useAssinaturaModulos.ts

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { mapError } from "../../../../core/errors/mapError";
import { assinaturaModulosApi } from "../api/assinaturaModulosApi";
import type { ModuloAssinatura } from "../tipos";

export function useAssinaturaModulos() {
  const [locatarioId, setLocatarioId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [modulos, setModulos] = useState<ModuloAssinatura[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [erro, setErro] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const carregar = useCallback(async () => {
    setIsLoading(true);
    setErro(null);

    try {
      const response = await assinaturaModulosApi.listar();
      setLocatarioId(response.locatarioId);
      setModulos(response.modulos);
    } catch (error) {
      setModulos([]);
      setErro(mapError(error).message);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    void carregar();
  }, [carregar]);

  return {
    locatarioId,
    modulos,
    isLoading,
    erro,
    recarregar: carregar,
  };
}

===== apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/assinatura/index.ts =====

===== apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/assinatura/pages/AssinaturaModulosPage.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/assinatura/pages/AssinaturaModulosPage.tsx

import { useAuthorization } from "../../../../core/auth/useAuthorization";
import { VincularModuloForm } from "../components/VincularModuloForm";
import { useAssinaturaModuloActions } from "../hooks/useAssinaturaModuloActions";
import { useAssinaturaModulos } from "../hooks/useAssinaturaModulos";

const ASSINATURAS_MODULE = "ASSINATURAS";

const ASSINATURAS_PERMISSIONS = {
  ATUALIZAR: "tenant.assinatura.atualizar",
} as const;

type ModuloAssinatura = {
  codigo: string;
  origem?: string | null;
};

type AssinaturaModulosTableProps = {
  modulos: ModuloAssinatura[];
  podeAtualizar: boolean;
  isRunning: boolean;
  onDesvincular: (moduloId: string) => void;
};

function AssinaturaModulosTableLocal({
  modulos,
  podeAtualizar,
  isRunning,
  onDesvincular,
}: AssinaturaModulosTableProps) {
  if (modulos.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-xl border bg-white p-4 text-sm text-slate-600">
        Nenhum módulo habilitado para este locatário.
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-2xl border bg-white">
      <table className="min-w-full border-collapse text-sm">
        <thead>
          <tr className="border-b bg-slate-50 text-left">
            <th className="px-4 py-3">Código</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3">Origem</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3">Ações</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>

        <tbody>
          {modulos.map((modulo) => (
            <tr key={modulo.codigo} className="border-b last:border-b-0">
              <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium">{modulo.codigo}</td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3">{modulo.origem ?? "—"}</td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3">
                {podeAtualizar ? (
                  <button
                    className="text-red-600 underline disabled:opacity-50"
                    type="button"
                    disabled={isRunning}
                    onClick={() => onDesvincular(modulo.codigo)}
                  >
                    Desvincular
                  </button>
                ) : (
                  <span className="text-slate-500">Sem permissão</span>
                )}
              </td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function AssinaturaModulosPage() {
  const { hasPermission, hasModule } = useAuthorization();

  const {
    locatarioId,
    modulos,
    isLoading,
    erro,
    recarregar,
  } = useAssinaturaModulos();

  const {
    vincular,
    desvincular,
    isRunning,
    erro: erroAcao,
    sucesso,
  } = useAssinaturaModuloActions({
    onSuccess: recarregar,
  });

  const podeAtualizar =
    hasModule(ASSINATURAS_MODULE) &&
    hasPermission(ASSINATURAS_PERMISSIONS.ATUALIZAR);

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-2xl border bg-white p-6">
        Carregando módulos da assinatura...
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-6">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">Módulos da assinatura</h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">
          Consulte os módulos habilitados para o locatário.
        </p>
      </div>

      {locatarioId ? (
        <div className="rounded-2xl border bg-white p-4 text-sm">
          <strong>Locatário:</strong> {locatarioId}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {erro ? (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
          {erro}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {erroAcao ? (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
          {erroAcao}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {sucesso ? (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-green-200 bg-green-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-green-700">
          {sucesso}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      <div className="flex gap-3">
        <button
          className="rounded-xl border px-4 py-2"
          type="button"
          onClick={() => void recarregar()}
        >
          Recarregar
        </button>
      </div>

      <AssinaturaModulosTableLocal
        modulos={modulos}
        podeAtualizar={podeAtualizar}
        isRunning={isRunning !== null}
        onDesvincular={(moduloId) => void desvincular(moduloId)}
      />

      {podeAtualizar ? (
        <VincularModuloForm
          isSaving={isRunning === "vincular"}
          onSubmit={(moduloId) => void vincular(moduloId)}
        />
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

===== apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/assinatura/tipos.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/assinatura/tipos.ts

export type ModuloAssinatura = {
  codigo: string;
  origem: string;
};

export type VincularModuloAssinaturaInput = {
  moduloId: string;
};

export type ListarMeusModulosAssinaturaResponse = {
  locatarioId: string;
  modulos: ModuloAssinatura[];
};

export type VinculoModuloAssinatura = {
  assinaturaId: string;
  moduloId: string;
  origem: string;
  gratuito: boolean;
  habilitadoEm: string;
  desabilitadoEm: string | null;
};

export type VincularModuloAssinaturaResponse = {
  item: VinculoModuloAssinatura;
};

export type DesvincularModuloAssinaturaResponse = {
  item: {
    assinaturaId: string;
    moduloId: string;
    desabilitadoEm: string;
  };
};

===== apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/clientes/api/clientesApi.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/clientes/api/clientesApi.ts

import { httpClient } from "../../../../core/api/httpClient";
import type { AtualizarClienteInput, AtualizarClienteResponse, CriarClienteInput, CriarClienteResponse, ExcluirClienteResponse, ListarClientesResponse, ObterClienteResponse } from "../tipos";

const BASE = "/clientes";

export const clientesApi = {
  listar(): Promise<ListarClientesResponse> {
    return httpClient.get<ListarClientesResponse>(BASE);
  },

  obter(clienteId: string): Promise<ObterClienteResponse> {
    return httpClient.get<ObterClienteResponse>(`${BASE}/${clienteId}`);
  },

  criar(input: CriarClienteInput): Promise<CriarClienteResponse> {
    return httpClient.post<CriarClienteResponse, CriarClienteInput>(BASE, input);
  },

  atualizar(clienteId: string, input: AtualizarClienteInput): Promise<AtualizarClienteResponse> {
    return httpClient.patch<AtualizarClienteResponse, AtualizarClienteInput>(`${BASE}/${clienteId}`, input);
  },

  excluir(clienteId: string): Promise<ExcluirClienteResponse> {
    return httpClient.delete<ExcluirClienteResponse>(`${BASE}/${clienteId}`);
  },
};

===== apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/clientes/components/ClienteForm.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/clientes/components/ClienteForm.tsx

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import type { AtualizarClienteInput, Cliente, CriarClienteInput } from "../tipos";

type Props = {
  mode: "create" | "edit";
  initialData?: Cliente | null;
  isSaving?: boolean;
  erro?: string | null;
  onSubmit: (input: CriarClienteInput | AtualizarClienteInput) => void | Promise<void>;
};

export function ClienteForm({ mode, initialData, isSaving, erro, onSubmit }: Props) {
  const [nome, setNome] = useState(initialData?.nome ?? "");
  const [documento, setDocumento] = useState(initialData?.documento ?? "");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState(initialData?.email ?? "");
  const [telefone, setTelefone] = useState(initialData?.telefone ?? "");

  const titulo = useMemo(
    () => (mode === "create" ? "Novo cliente" : "Editar cliente"),
    [mode],
  );

  async function handleSubmit(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();

    await onSubmit({
      nome,
      documento: documento || null,
      email: email || null,
      telefone: telefone || null,
    });
  }

  return (
    <form className="grid gap-4 rounded-2xl border bg-white p-6" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
      <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">{titulo}</h2>

      {erro ? (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-red-700">
          {erro}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      <label className="grid gap-1">
        <span className="text-sm font-medium">Nome</span>
        <input
          className="rounded-xl border px-3 py-2"
          value={nome}
          onChange={(event) => setNome(event.target.value)}
          required
        />
      </label>

      <label className="grid gap-1">
        <span className="text-sm font-medium">Documento</span>
        <input
          className="rounded-xl border px-3 py-2"
          value={documento}
          onChange={(event) => setDocumento(event.target.value)}
        />
      </label>

      <label className="grid gap-1">
        <span className="text-sm font-medium">E-mail</span>
        <input
          className="rounded-xl border px-3 py-2"
          type="email"
          value={email}
          onChange={(event) => setEmail(event.target.value)}
        />
      </label>

      <label className="grid gap-1">
        <span className="text-sm font-medium">Telefone</span>
        <input
          className="rounded-xl border px-3 py-2"
          value={telefone}
          onChange={(event) => setTelefone(event.target.value)}
        />
      </label>

      <div className="flex justify-end">
        <button
          className="rounded-xl border px-4 py-2 font-medium"
          type="submit"
          disabled={Boolean(isSaving)}
        >
          {isSaving ? "Salvando..." : "Salvar"}
        </button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

===== apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/clientes/components/ClientesTable.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/clientes/components/ClientesTable.tsx

import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import type { Cliente } from "../tipos";

type Props = {
  itens: Cliente[];
};

function formatarData(value?: string | null): string {
  if (!value) return "—";
  const date = new Date(value);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return value;
  return date.toLocaleDateString("pt-BR");
}

function codigoCurto(id: string): string {
  return id.slice(0, 8).toUpperCase();
}

export function ClientesTable({ itens }: Props) {
  if (itens.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="ck-register-empty">
        <div className="ck-register-empty-title">Nenhum cliente encontrado</div>
        <p className="ck-register-empty-desc">
          Cadastre clientes para agilizar novas vendas e manter histórico.
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="hidden overflow-x-auto md:block">
        <div className="ck-register-table-panel">
          <table className="ck-register-table">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th>Cliente</th>
                <th>Documento</th>
                <th>Contato</th>
                <th>Cadastrado em</th>
                <th>Código</th>
                <th>Ações</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>

            <tbody>
              {itens.map((cliente) => (
                <tr key={cliente.clienteId}>
                  <td>
                    <div className="ck-register-main">{cliente.nome}</div>
                    <div className="ck-register-sub">{cliente.email ?? "Sem e-mail"}</div>
                  </td>
                  <td>{cliente.documento ?? "—"}</td>
                  <td>{cliente.telefone ?? "—"}</td>
                  <td>{formatarData(cliente.criadoEm)}</td>
                  <td>
                    <span className="ck-register-pill">
                      #{codigoCurto(cliente.clienteId)}
                    </span>
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    <Link
                      className="ck-register-link"
                      to={`/app/clientes/${cliente.clienteId}`}
                    >
                      Detalhes
                    </Link>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="ck-register-card-list md:hidden">
        {itens.map((cliente) => (
          <article className="ck-register-card" key={cliente.clienteId}>
            <div className="ck-register-card-top">
              <div>
                <div className="ck-register-card-title">{cliente.nome}</div>
                <div className="ck-register-card-desc">
                  {cliente.documento ?? "Sem documento"}
                </div>
              </div>

              <span className="ck-register-pill">#{codigoCurto(cliente.clienteId)}</span>
            </div>

            <div className="ck-register-card-grid">
              <div>
                <div className="ck-register-label">E-mail</div>
                <div className="ck-register-value">{cliente.email ?? "—"}</div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <div className="ck-register-label">Telefone</div>
                <div className="ck-register-value">{cliente.telefone ?? "—"}</div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <Link
              className="ck-register-action-secondary"
              to={`/app/clientes/${cliente.clienteId}`}
            >
              Ver detalhes
            </Link>
          </article>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


===== apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/clientes/hooks/useClienteActions.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/clientes/hooks/useClienteActions.ts

import { useState } from "react";
import { mapError } from "../../../../core/errors/mapError";
import { clientesApi } from "../api/clientesApi";

type Params = {
  onSuccess?: () => void | Promise<void>;
};

export function useClienteActions({ onSuccess }: Params = {}) {
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [erro, setErro] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function excluir(clienteId: string) {
    const id = clienteId.trim();

    if (!id) {
      setErro("ID do cliente inválido.");
      return false;
    }

    setIsSubmitting(true);
    setErro(null);

    try {
      await clientesApi.excluir(id);
      await onSuccess?.();
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      setErro(mapError(error).message);
      return false;
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  return {
    excluir,
    isSubmitting,
    erro,
  };
}

===== apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/clientes/hooks/useClienteDetails.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/clientes/hooks/useClienteDetails.ts

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { mapError } from "../../../../core/errors/mapError";
import { clientesApi } from "../api/clientesApi";
import type { Cliente } from "../tipos";

export function useClienteDetails(clienteId?: string) {
  const [cliente, setCliente] = useState<Cliente | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [erro, setErro] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let ativo = true;

    async function carregar() {
      if (!clienteId?.trim()) {
        setErro("ID do cliente inválido.");
        setIsLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      setIsLoading(true);
      setErro(null);

      try {
        const response = await clientesApi.obter(clienteId);

        if (ativo) {
          setCliente(response.item);
        }
      } catch (error) {
        if (ativo) {
          setErro(mapError(error).message);
        }
      } finally {
        if (ativo) {
          setIsLoading(false);
        }
      }
    }

    void carregar();

    return () => {
      ativo = false;
    };
  }, [clienteId]);

  return {
    cliente,
    isLoading,
    erro,
  };
}

===== apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/clientes/hooks/useClientes.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/clientes/hooks/useClientes.ts

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { mapError } from "../../../../core/errors/mapError";
import { clientesApi } from "../api/clientesApi";
import type { Cliente } from "../tipos";

function filtrarClientes(clientes: Cliente[], busca: string): Cliente[] {
  const termo = busca.trim().toLowerCase();

  if (!termo) return clientes;

  return clientes.filter((cliente) =>
    cliente.nome.toLowerCase().includes(termo) ||
    cliente.documento?.toLowerCase().includes(termo) ||
    cliente.email?.toLowerCase().includes(termo),
  );
}

export function useClientes() {
  const [clientes, setClientes] = useState<Cliente[]>([]);
  const [busca, setBusca] = useState("");
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [erro, setErro] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const carregar = useCallback(async () => {
    setIsLoading(true);
    setErro(null);

    try {
      const response = await clientesApi.listar();
      setClientes(response.itens);
    } catch (error) {
      setErro(mapError(error).message);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    void carregar();
  }, [carregar]);

  const clientesFiltrados = useMemo(
    () => filtrarClientes(clientes, busca),
    [clientes, busca],
  );

  return {
    clientes,
    clientesFiltrados,
    busca,
    setBusca,
    isLoading,
    erro,
    recarregar: carregar,
  };
}

===== apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/clientes/hooks/useSalvarCliente.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/clientes/hooks/useSalvarCliente.ts

import { useState } from "react";
import { mapError } from "../../../../core/errors/mapError";
import { clientesApi } from "../api/clientesApi";
import type { AtualizarClienteInput, CriarClienteInput } from "../tipos";

type Params = {
  mode: "create" | "edit";
  clienteId?: string;
  onSuccess?: () => void | Promise<void>;
};

export function useSalvarCliente({ mode, clienteId, onSuccess }: Params) {
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);
  const [erro, setErro] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function salvar(input: CriarClienteInput | AtualizarClienteInput) {
    setIsSaving(true);
    setErro(null);

    try {
      if (mode === "create") {
        await clientesApi.criar(input as CriarClienteInput);
      } else {
        const id = clienteId?.trim();

        if (!id) {
          setErro("ID do cliente inválido.");
          return false;
        }

        await clientesApi.atualizar(id, input as AtualizarClienteInput);
      }

      await onSuccess?.();
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      setErro(mapError(error).message);
      return false;
    } finally {
      setIsSaving(false);
    }
  }

  return {
    salvar,
    isSaving,
    erro,
  };
}

// apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/clientes/pages/ClienteDetailsPage.tsx

import { Link, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { useClienteDetails } from "../hooks/useClienteDetails";

export default function ClienteDetailsPage() {
  const params = useParams<{ clienteId: string }>();
  const clienteId = params.clienteId?.trim();

  const { cliente, isLoading, erro } = useClienteDetails(clienteId);

  if (isLoading) {
    return <div className="rounded-2xl border bg-white p-6">Carregando cliente...</div>;
  }

  if (erro) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
        {erro}
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!cliente) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
        Cliente não encontrado.
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-6">
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">{cliente.nome}</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">
            {cliente.documento ?? "Sem documento"}
          </p>
        </div>

        <Link
          className="rounded-xl border px-4 py-2"
          to={`/app/clientes/${cliente.clienteId}/editar`}
        >
          Editar
        </Link>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-3 rounded-2xl border bg-white p-6 text-sm">
        <div><strong>ID:</strong> {cliente.clienteId}</div>
        <div><strong>Nome:</strong> {cliente.nome}</div>
        <div><strong>Documento:</strong> {cliente.documento ?? "—"}</div>
        <div><strong>E-mail:</strong> {cliente.email ?? "—"}</div>
        <div><strong>Telefone:</strong> {cliente.telefone ?? "—"}</div>
        <div><strong>Criado em:</strong> {cliente.criadoEm ?? "—"}</div>
        <div><strong>Atualizado em:</strong> {cliente.atualizadoEm ?? "—"}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { Link } from "react-router-dom";

function contarComEmail<T extends { email?: string | null }>(itens: T[]): number {
  return itens.filter((item) => Boolean(item.email?.trim())).length;
}

function contarComDocumento<T extends { documento?: string | null }>(itens: T[]): number {
  return itens.filter((item) => Boolean(item.documento?.trim())).length;
}

export default function ClientesListPage() {

===== apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/clientes/pages/EditarClientePage.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/clientes/pages/EditarClientePage.tsx

import { useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { ClienteForm } from "../components/ClienteForm";
import { useClienteDetails } from "../hooks/useClienteDetails";
import { useSalvarCliente } from "../hooks/useSalvarCliente";
import type { AtualizarClienteInput, CriarClienteInput } from "../tipos";

export default function EditarClientePage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const params = useParams<{ clienteId: string }>();
  const clienteId = params.clienteId?.trim();

  const { cliente, isLoading, erro: erroCarga } = useClienteDetails(clienteId);

  const { salvar, isSaving, erro } = useSalvarCliente({
    mode: "edit",
    clienteId,
    onSuccess: () => navigate(clienteId ? `/app/clientes/${clienteId}` : "/app/clientes"),
  });

  async function handleSubmit(input: CriarClienteInput | AtualizarClienteInput) {
    await salvar(input);
  }

  if (isLoading) {
    return <div className="rounded-2xl border bg-white p-6">Carregando cliente...</div>;
  }

  if (erroCarga) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
        {erroCarga}
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!cliente) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
        Cliente não encontrado.
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-6">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">Editar cliente</h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">
          Atualize os dados do cliente.
        </p>
      </div>

      <ClienteForm
        mode="edit"
        initialData={cliente}
        isSaving={isSaving}
        erro={erro}
        onSubmit={handleSubmit}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

===== apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/clientes/pages/NovoClientePage.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/clientes/pages/NovoClientePage.tsx

import { useState } from "react";
import { clientesApi } from "../api/clientesApi";

export default function NovoClientePage() {
  const [nome, setNome] = useState("");
  const [documento, setDocumento] = useState("");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [telefone, setTelefone] = useState("");
  const [erro, setErro] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [sucesso, setSucesso] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function handleSubmit(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setErro(null);
    setSucesso(null);

    try {
      await clientesApi.criar({
        nome,
        documento: documento || null,
        email: email || null,
        telefone: telefone || null,
      });

      setSucesso("Cliente criado com sucesso.");
      setNome("");
      setDocumento("");
      setEmail("");
      setTelefone("");
    } catch (error) {
      setErro(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Erro ao criar cliente.");
    }
  }

  return (
    <form className="grid gap-4" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
      <h1>Novo cliente</h1>

      {erro ? <div>{erro}</div> : null}
      {sucesso ? <div>{sucesso}</div> : null}

      <input value={nome} onChange={(e) => setNome(e.target.value)} placeholder="Nome" required />
      <input value={documento} onChange={(e) => setDocumento(e.target.value)} placeholder="Documento" />
      <input value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} placeholder="E-mail" type="email" />
      <input value={telefone} onChange={(e) => setTelefone(e.target.value)} placeholder="Telefone" />

      <button type="submit">Salvar</button>
    </form>
  );
}

export type Cliente = {
  clienteId: string;
  locatarioId: string;
  nome: string;
  documento: string | null;
  email?: string | null;
  telefone?: string | null;
  criadoEm?: string;
  atualizadoEm?: string;
  excluidoEm?: string | null;
};

export type ListarClientesResponse = {
  itens: Cliente[];
};

export type ObterClienteResponse = {
  item: Cliente;
};

export type CriarClienteResponse = {
  item: Cliente;
};

export type AtualizarClienteResponse = {
  item: Cliente;
};

export type ExcluirClienteResponse = void;

export type CriarClienteInput = {

  documento?: string | null;
  email?: string | null;
  telefone?: string | null;

export type AtualizarClienteInput = Partial<CriarClienteInput>;
