export default function EditarUsuarioPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const params = useParams<{ usuarioId: string }>();
  const usuarioId = params.usuarioId?.trim();

  const [usuario, setUsuario] = useState<Usuario | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [erroCarga, setErroCarga] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const { salvar, isSaving, erro } = useSalvarUsuario({
    mode: "edit",
    usuarioId,
    onSuccess: () => {
      navigate(usuarioId ? `/app/usuarios/${usuarioId}` : "/app/usuarios");
    },
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    let ativo = true;

    async function carregar() {
      if (!usuarioId) {
        if (ativo) {
          setUsuario(null);
          setErroCarga("ID do usuário inválido.");
          setIsLoading(false);
        }
        return;
      }

      setIsLoading(true);
      setErroCarga(null);

      try {
        const response = await usuariosApi.obter(usuarioId);

        if (ativo) {
          setUsuario(response.item);
        }
      } catch {
        if (ativo) {
          setUsuario(null);
          setErroCarga("Não foi possível carregar o usuário.");
        }
      } finally {
        if (ativo) {
          setIsLoading(false);
        }
      }
    }

    void carregar();

    return () => {
      ativo = false;
    };
  }, [usuarioId]);

  async function handleSubmit(input: AtualizarUsuarioInput) {
    await salvar(input);
  }

  if (isLoading) {
    return <div className="rounded-2xl border bg-white p-6">Carregando usuário...</div>;
  }

  if (erroCarga) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-red-700">
        {erroCarga}
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!usuario) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-red-700">
        Usuário não encontrado.
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-6">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">Editar usuário</h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">
          Atualize nome e e-mail do usuário.
        </p>
      </div>

      <PageGuard
        requiredModule={USUARIOS_MODULE}
        requiredPermissions={[USUARIOS_PERMISSIONS.ATUALIZAR]}
      >
        <UsuarioForm
          mode="edit"
          initialData={usuario}
          isSaving={isSaving}
          erro={erro}
          onSubmit={handleSubmit}
        />
      </PageGuard>
    </div>
  );
}


===== apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/usuarios/pages/minhaSenhaPage.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/usuarios/pages/minhaSenhaPage.tsx

import { useAuth } from "../../../../core/auth/useAuth";
import { UsuarioSenhaForm } from "../components/UsuarioSenhaForm";
import { useSenhaUsuario } from "../hooks/useSenhaUsuario";

export default function MinhaSenhaPage() {
  const { user } = useAuth();

  const {
    alterarMinhaSenha,
    erro,
    sucesso,
    isSaving,
  } = useSenhaUsuario({
    usuarioId: user?.usuarioId,
  });

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-6">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">Minha senha</h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">
          Altere a sua senha de acesso.
        </p>
      </div>

      <UsuarioSenhaForm
        mode="alterar"
        isSaving={isSaving}
        erro={erro}
        sucesso={sucesso}
        onSubmit={async (input) => {
          await alterarMinhaSenha(input as { senhaAtual: string; novaSenha: string });
        }}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

===== apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/usuarios/pages/usuarioDetailsPage.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/usuarios/pages/usuarioDetailsPage.tsx

//     </div>
//   );

export default function UsuarioDetailsPage() {
  const params = useParams<{ usuarioId: string }>();
  const usuarioId = params.usuarioId?.trim();

  const { usuario, isLoading, erro } = useUsuarioDetails(usuarioId);

  const {
    redefinirSenha,
    erro: erroSenha,
    sucesso: sucessoSenha,
    isSaving: isSavingSenha,
  } = useSenhaUsuario({
    usuarioId,
  });

  async function handleRedefinirSenha(input: RedefinirSenhaUsuarioInput) {
    await redefinirSenha(input);
  }

  if (isLoading) {
    return <div className="rounded-2xl border bg-white p-6">Carregando usuário...</div>;
  }

  if (erro) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
        {erro}
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!usuario) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
        Usuário não encontrado.
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-6">
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">{usuario.nome}</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">{usuario.email}</p>
        </div>

        <PageGuard
          requiredModule={USUARIOS_MODULE}
          requiredPermissions={[USUARIOS_PERMISSIONS.ATUALIZAR]}
          fallback={
            <div className="rounded-xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-amber-800">
              Você pode visualizar, mas não editar este usuário.
            </div>
          }
        >
          <Link
            className="rounded-xl border px-4 py-2"
            to={`/app/usuarios/${usuario.usuarioId}/editar`}
          >
            Editar
          </Link>
        </PageGuard>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-3 rounded-2xl border bg-white p-6 text-sm">
        <div><strong>ID:</strong> {usuario.usuarioId}</div>
        <div><strong>Nome:</strong> {usuario.nome}</div>
        <div><strong>E-mail:</strong> {usuario.email}</div>
        <div><strong>Status:</strong> {usuario.status}</div>
        <div><strong>Último login:</strong> {usuario.ultimoLoginEm ?? "-"}</div>
        <div><strong>Criado em:</strong> {usuario.criadoEm}</div>
        <div><strong>Atualizado em:</strong> {usuario.atualizadoEm}</div>
      </div>

      <PageGuard
        requiredModule={USUARIOS_MODULE}
        requiredPermissions={[USUARIOS_PERMISSIONS.ATUALIZAR]}
        fallback={
          <div className="rounded-xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-amber-800">
            Você não possui permissão para redefinir senha.
          </div>
        }
      >
        <UsuarioSenhaForm
          mode="redefinir"
          isSaving={isSavingSenha}
          erro={erroSenha}
          sucesso={sucessoSenha}
          onSubmit={handleRedefinirSenha}
        />
      </PageGuard>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function UsuariosListPage() {
  const { usuariosFiltrados, isLoading, erro, recarregar } = useUsuarios();

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">Usuários</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">
            Gerencie os usuários do locatário.
          </p>
        </div>

        <PageGuard
          requiredModule={USUARIOS_MODULE}
          requiredPermissions={[USUARIOS_PERMISSIONS.CRIAR]}
          fallback={
            <div className="rounded-xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-amber-800">
              Você não possui permissão para criar usuários.
            </div>
          }
        >
          <Link
            className="rounded-xl border px-4 py-2"
            to="/app/usuarios/novo"
          >
            Novo usuário
          </Link>
        </PageGuard>
      </div>

      {erro ? (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
          {erro}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      <div className="flex gap-3">
        <button
          className="rounded-xl border px-4 py-2"
          type="button"
          onClick={() => void recarregar()}
        >
          Recarregar
        </button>
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="rounded-2xl border bg-white p-6">Carregando usuários...</div>
      ) : (
        <UsuariosTable itens={usuariosFiltrados} />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/usuarios/README.md =====


# Módulo frontend de usuários

Estrutura criada seguindo o padrão atual do frontend:

- `api/usuariosApi.ts`
- `components/`
- `hooks/`
- `pages/`
- `types.ts`

## Fluxos cobertos
- listagem
- detalhe
- criação
- edição
- inativação
- reativação

## Observações
- o backend atual ainda expõe `senhaHash` no DTO; esta implementação não usa esse campo
- ideal alinhar também o catálogo de status do backend antes de consolidar a UI


===== apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/usuarios/tipos.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/usuarios/tipos.ts

export type StatusUsuario = "ATIVO" | "INATIVO" | "SUSPENSO" | "ENCERRADO";

export type Usuario = {
  usuarioId: string;
  locatarioId: string | null;
  email: string;
  nome: string;
  status: StatusUsuario;
  ultimoLoginEm: string | null;
  criadoEm: string;
  atualizadoEm: string;
  excluidoEm: string | null;
};

export type ListarUsuariosResponse = {
  itens: Usuario[];
};

export type ObterUsuarioResponse = {
  item: Usuario;
};

export type CriarUsuarioInput = {
  email: string;
  nome: string;
  senha: string;
};

export type CriarUsuarioResponse = {
  item: {
    usuarioId: string;
  };
};

export type AtualizarUsuarioInput = {
  email?: string;
  nome?: string;
};

export type AtualizarUsuarioResponse = {
  item: Usuario;
};

export type AcaoUsuarioResponse = {
  item: Usuario;
};

export type FiltroUsuarios = {
  busca?: string;
  status?: StatusUsuario | "TODOS";
};

export type UsuarioFormMode = "create" | "edit";

export type AlterarMinhaSenhaInput = {
  senhaAtual: string;
  novaSenha: string;
};

export type RedefinirSenhaUsuarioInput = {
  novaSenha: string;
};

export type AlterarSenhaUsuarioResponse = {
  item: {
    usuarioId: string;
  };
};

export type RedefinirSenhaUsuarioResponse = {
  item: {
    usuarioId: string;
  };
};

// apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/vendas/api/vendasApi.ts

import { CriarTransacaoResponse, transacoesApi } from "../../transacoes";
import type { CriarVendaInput } from "../tipos";

const BASE = "/vendas";

export const vendasApi = {
  criar(input: CriarVendaInput): Promise<CriarTransacaoResponse> {
    return transacoesApi.criarVenda(input);
  },
};

===== apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/vendas/pages/NovaVendaPage.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/vendas/pages/NovaVendaPage.tsx

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Link, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { mapError } from "../../../../core/errors/mapError";
import { clientesApi } from "../../clientes/api/clientesApi";
import type { Cliente } from "../../clientes/tipos";
import { vendasApi } from "../api/vendasApi";
import "../../transacoes/pages/transacoes.css";

function labelCliente(cliente: Cliente): string {
  return cliente.documento ? `${cliente.nome} — ${cliente.documento}` : cliente.nome;
}

function textoBuscaCliente(cliente: Cliente): string {
  return [cliente.nome, cliente.documento, cliente.email, cliente.telefone]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(" ")
    .toLowerCase();
}

export default function NovaVendaPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [clientes, setClientes] = useState<Cliente[]>([]);
  const [clienteId, setClienteId] = useState("");
  const [buscaCliente, setBuscaCliente] = useState("");
  const [observacao, setObservacao] = useState("");
  const [erro, setErro] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [erroClientes, setErroClientes] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isLoadingClientes, setIsLoadingClientes] = useState(true);
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    let ativo = true;

    async function carregarClientes() {
      setIsLoadingClientes(true);
      setErroClientes(null);

      try {
        const response = await clientesApi.listar();

        if (ativo) setClientes(response.itens);
      } catch (error) {
        if (ativo) setErroClientes(mapError(error).message);
      } finally {
        if (ativo) setIsLoadingClientes(false);
      }
    }

    void carregarClientes();

    return () => {
      ativo = false;
    };
  }, []);

  const clientesFiltrados = useMemo(() => {
    const termo = buscaCliente.trim().toLowerCase();

    if (!termo) return clientes;

    return clientes.filter((cliente) => textoBuscaCliente(cliente).includes(termo));
  }, [clientes, buscaCliente]);

  const clienteSelecionado = clientes.find(
    (cliente) => cliente.clienteId === clienteId,
  );

  async function handleSubmit(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();

    setErro(null);
    setIsSaving(true);

    try {
      const response = await vendasApi.criar({
        clienteId: clienteId.trim() || null,
        observacao: observacao.trim() || null,
      });

      navigate(`/app/transacoes/${response.item.transacaoId}`);
    } catch (error) {
      setErro(mapError(error).message);
    } finally {
      setIsSaving(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <form className="ck-page" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
      <section className="ck-page-hero">
        <div className="ck-page-hero-inner">
          <div>
            <div className="ck-page-kicker">Vendas</div>
            <h1 className="ck-page-title">Nova venda</h1>
            <p className="ck-page-description">
              Crie a venda e depois adicione os produtos na tela de detalhes.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="ck-page-actions">
            <Link className="ck-action-secondary" to="/app/transacoes">
              Voltar
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {erro ? <div className="ck-alert">{erro}</div> : null}

      {erroClientes ? (
        <div className="ck-alert ck-alert-warning">
          Não foi possível carregar clientes: {erroClientes}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      <section className="ck-form-card">
        <div className="ck-form-card-header">
          <div className="ck-form-card-title">Cliente</div>
          <p className="ck-form-card-desc">
            Busque por nome, documento, e-mail ou telefone.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="ck-form-card-body">
          <label className="ck-field">
            <span>Buscar cliente</span>
            <input
              className="ck-input"
              placeholder="Ex.: Maria, CPF/CNPJ, e-mail..."
              value={buscaCliente}
              onChange={(event) => setBuscaCliente(event.target.value)}
            />
          </label>

          <label className="ck-field">
            <span>Cliente selecionado</span>
            <select
              className="ck-select"
              value={clienteId}
              onChange={(event) => setClienteId(event.target.value)}
              disabled={isLoadingClientes}
            >
              <option value="">
                {isLoadingClientes ? "Carregando clientes..." : "Venda sem cliente"}
              </option>

              {clientesFiltrados.map((cliente) => (
                <option key={cliente.clienteId} value={cliente.clienteId}>
                  {labelCliente(cliente)}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </label>

          {clienteSelecionado ? (
            <div className="ck-selected-box">
              <div className="ck-selected-grid">
                <div>
                  <div className="ck-selected-label">Nome</div>
                  <div className="ck-selected-value">{clienteSelecionado.nome}</div>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <div className="ck-selected-label">Documento</div>
                  <div className="ck-selected-value">{clienteSelecionado.documento ?? "—"}</div>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <div className="ck-selected-label">E-mail</div>
                  <div className="ck-selected-value">{clienteSelecionado.email ?? "—"}</div>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <div className="ck-selected-label">Telefone</div>
                  <div className="ck-selected-value">{clienteSelecionado.telefone ?? "—"}</div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="ck-form-card">
        <div className="ck-form-card-header">
          <div className="ck-form-card-title">Observação</div>
          <p className="ck-form-card-desc">
            Informação opcional para facilitar o histórico.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="ck-form-card-body">
          <label className="ck-field">
            <span>Observação da venda</span>
            <textarea
              className="ck-input ck-textarea"
              value={observacao}
              onChange={(event) => setObservacao(event.target.value)}
              placeholder="Ex.: venda balcão, pedido especial, condições combinadas..."
            />
          </label>
        </div>
      </section>

      <div className="flex justify-end">
        <button className="ck-action-primary" disabled={isSaving} type="submit">
          {isSaving ? "Criando..." : "Criar venda e adicionar itens"}
        </button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

export type CriarVendaInput = {
  clienteId?: string | null;

===== apps/web/src/layouts/authLayout.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/layouts/authLayout.tsx

import type { JSX } from "react";
import { Navigate, Outlet } from "react-router-dom";
import { isPlataforma } from "../core/auth/tipos";
import { useAuth } from "../core/auth/useAuth";
import "../features/auth/pages/login.css";

export default function AuthLayout(): JSX.Element {
  const { isLoading, isAuthenticated, user } = useAuth();

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="ck-auth-page">
        <div className="ck-auth-loading">Carregando...</div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (isAuthenticated && user) {
    return (
      <Navigate to={isPlataforma(user) ? "/plataforma" : "/app"} replace />
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="ck-auth-page">
      <Outlet />
    </div>
  );
}


===== apps/web/src/layouts/superAdminLayout.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/layouts/superAdminLayout.tsx

===== apps/web/src/shared/layout/AppBreadcrumb.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/shared/layout/AppBreadcrumb.tsx

import type { JSX } from "react";
import { Link, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";

type AppBreadcrumbProps = {
  variant: "tenant" | "plataforma";
};

const LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  app: "Início",
  plataforma: "Plataforma",
  usuarios: "Usuários",
  locatario: "Locatário",
  locatarios: "Locatários",
  configuracoes: "Configurações",
  assinatura: "Assinatura",
  modulos: "Módulos",
  clientes: "Clientes",
  fornecedores: "Fornecedores",
  produtos: "Produtos",
  transacoes: "Transações",
  vendas: "Vendas",
  compras: "Compras",
  nova: "Nova",
  novo: "Novo",
  editar: "Editar",
  "minha-senha": "Minha senha",
};

function isLikelyId(segment: string): boolean {
  return (
    segment.length >= 16 ||
    /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}/i.test(segment)
  );
}

function formatSegment(segment: string): string {
  const known = LABELS[segment];

  if (known) return known;

  if (isLikelyId(segment)) {
    return `#${segment.slice(0, 8).toUpperCase()}`;
  }

  return segment
    .split("-")
    .filter(Boolean)
    .map((part) => part.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + part.slice(1))
    .join(" ");
}

export function AppBreadcrumb({ variant }: AppBreadcrumbProps): JSX.Element {
  const location = useLocation();

  const segments = location.pathname
    .split("/")
    .map((segment) => segment.trim())
    .filter(Boolean);

  const rootPath = variant === "plataforma" ? "/plataforma" : "/app";

  if (segments.length <= 1) {
    return (
      <nav aria-label="Breadcrumb" className="text-sm text-slate-500">
        <span>{variant === "plataforma" ? "Plataforma" : "Início"}</span>
      </nav>
    );
  }

  return (
    <nav aria-label="Breadcrumb" className="text-sm text-slate-500">
      <ol className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        {segments.map((segment, index) => {
          const path = `/${segments.slice(0, index + 1).join("/")}`;
          const isLast = index === segments.length - 1;
          const label =
            index === 0
              ? variant === "plataforma"
                ? "Plataforma"
                : "Início"
              : formatSegment(segment);

          if (index === 0 && path !== rootPath) {
            return null;
          }

          return (
            <li key={`${path}-${segment}`} className="flex items-center gap-2">
              {index > 0 ? <span className="text-slate-300">/</span> : null}

              {isLast ? (
                <span className="font-medium text-slate-700">{label}</span>
              ) : (
                <Link className="hover:text-slate-900 hover:underline" to={path}>
                  {label}
                </Link>
              )}
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ol>
    </nav>
  );
}

// apps/web/src/shared/layout/AppFooter.tsx

import type { JSX } from "react";

type AppFooterProps = {

===== apps/web/src/shared/layout/AppHeader.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/shared/layout/AppHeader.tsx

import type { JSX } from "react";
import { NavLink } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAuth } from "../../core/auth/useAuth";
import { useTenant } from "../../core/tenant/useTenant";

export type AppNavItem = {
  label: string;
  to: string;
  end?: boolean;
  icon?: string;
};

type AppHeaderProps = {
  variant: "tenant" | "plataforma";
  navItems: AppNavItem[];
};

const APP_ENV = import.meta.env.VITE_APP_ENV ?? "Beta";

function userLabel(user: ReturnType<typeof useAuth>["user"]): string {
  return (
    user?.nome?.trim() ||
    user?.email?.trim() ||
    user?.usuarioId?.slice(0, 8) ||
    "Usuário"
  );
}

function userInitial(user: ReturnType<typeof useAuth>["user"]): string {
  const label = userLabel(user).trim();
  return label.charAt(0).toUpperCase() || "U";
}

export function AppHeader({ variant, navItems }: AppHeaderProps): JSX.Element {
  const { user, logout } = useAuth();
  const { branding } = useTenant();

  const isPlataforma = variant === "plataforma";

  const title = isPlataforma
    ? "Core-kit Plataforma"
    : branding?.nomeFantasia?.trim() || "Core-kit";

  const subtitle = isPlataforma
    ? "Painel de administração do SaaS"
    : "Painel operacional do locatário";

  async function handleLogout() {
    await logout();
  }

  return (
    <header className="ck-header">
      <div className="ck-header-inner">
        <div className="ck-brand">
          {variant === "tenant" && branding?.logoUrl ? (
            <img alt={title} className="ck-brand-logo" src={branding.logoUrl} />
          ) : (
            <div className="ck-brand-mark">CK</div>
          )}

          <div>
            <div className="ck-brand-title-row">
              <strong className="ck-brand-title">{title}</strong>
              <span className="ck-beta-badge">{APP_ENV}</span>
            </div>

            <p className="ck-brand-subtitle">{subtitle}</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="ck-header-actions">
          <div className="ck-search-pill">⌕ Busca global em breve</div>

          <div className="ck-user-card">
            <div className="ck-user-avatar">{userInitial(user)}</div>

            <div className="ck-user-meta">
              <div className="ck-user-name">{userLabel(user)}</div>
              <div className="ck-user-role">
                {isPlataforma ? "Plataforma" : "Locatário"}
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              className="ck-logout-button"
              type="button"
              onClick={() => void handleLogout()}
            >
              Sair
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <nav className="ck-mobile-nav">
        {navItems.map((item) => (
          <NavLink
            key={item.to}
            className="ck-mobile-nav-link"
            end={item.end}
            to={item.to}
          >
            <span>{item.icon ?? "•"}</span>
            <span>{item.label}</span>
          </NavLink>
        ))}
      </nav>
    </header>
  );
}


===== apps/web/src/shared/layout/AppShell.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/shared/layout/AppShell.tsx

import type { JSX, PropsWithChildren } from "react";
import "./appShell.css";
import { AppBreadcrumb } from "./AppBreadcrumb";
import { AppFooter } from "./AppFooter";
import { AppHeader, type AppNavItem } from "./AppHeader";
import { AppSidebar } from "./AppSidebar";

type AppShellProps = PropsWithChildren<{
  variant: "tenant" | "plataforma";
  navItems: AppNavItem[];
}>;

export function AppShell({
  children,
  variant,
  navItems,
}: AppShellProps): JSX.Element {
  return (
    <div className="ck-shell">
      <AppHeader navItems={navItems} variant={variant} />

      <div className="ck-body">
        <AppSidebar navItems={navItems} variant={variant} />

        <main className="ck-main">
          <div className="ck-breadcrumb-wrap">
            <AppBreadcrumb variant={variant} />
          </div>

          {children}
        </main>
      </div>

      <AppFooter variant={variant} />
    </div>
  );
}


===== apps/web/src/shared/layout/AppSidebar.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/shared/layout/AppSidebar.tsx

import type { JSX } from "react";
import { NavLink } from "react-router-dom";
import type { AppNavItem } from "./AppHeader";

type AppSidebarProps = {
  variant: "tenant" | "plataforma";
  navItems: AppNavItem[];
};

export function AppSidebar({
  variant,
  navItems,
}: AppSidebarProps): JSX.Element {
  return (
    <aside className="ck-sidebar">
      <div className="ck-sidebar-panel">
        <div className="ck-sidebar-top">
          <div className="ck-sidebar-kicker">
            {variant === "plataforma" ? "Administração" : "Operação"}
          </div>

          <div className="ck-sidebar-title">
            {variant === "plataforma" ? "Plataforma" : "Módulos do sistema"}
          </div>

          <p className="ck-sidebar-desc">
            {variant === "plataforma"
              ? "Gerencie locatários, módulos e usuários da plataforma."
              : "Acesse rapidamente as áreas principais do locatário."}
          </p>
        </div>

        <nav className="ck-sidebar-nav">
          {navItems.map((item) => (
            <NavLink
              key={item.to}
              className="ck-sidebar-link"
              end={item.end}
              to={item.to}
            >
              <span className="ck-sidebar-icon">{item.icon ?? "•"}</span>
              <span>{item.label}</span>
            </NavLink>
          ))}
        </nav>

        <div className="ck-sidebar-footer">
          <strong>Core-kit Beta</strong>
          <br />
          Interface em evolução para PWA, mobile e operação diária.
        </div>
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
}

===== apps/web/src/shared/ui/Alert.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/shared/ui/Alert.tsx

import type { JSX, PropsWithChildren } from "react";

type AlertVariant = "error" | "warning" | "success" | "info";
type AlertProps = PropsWithChildren<{ variant?: AlertVariant }>;

function variantClass(variant: AlertVariant): string {
  switch (variant) {
    case "error": return "border-red-200 bg-red-50 text-red-700";
    case "warning": return "border-yellow-200 bg-yellow-50 text-yellow-800";
    case "success": return "border-green-200 bg-green-50 text-green-700";
    case "info": default: return "border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 text-blue-700";
  }
}

export function Alert({ children, variant = "info" }: AlertProps): JSX.Element {
  return <div className={["rounded-xl border px-4 py-3 text-sm", variantClass(variant)].join(" ")}>{children}</div>;
}


===== apps/web/src/shared/ui/Button.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/shared/ui/Button.tsx

import type { ButtonHTMLAttributes, JSX, PropsWithChildren } from "react";

type ButtonVariant = "primary" | "secondary" | "danger" | "ghost";

type ButtonProps = PropsWithChildren<ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> & { variant?: ButtonVariant }>;

function variantClass(variant: ButtonVariant): string {
  switch (variant) {
    case "primary": return "border-transparent bg-slate-900 text-white hover:bg-slate-800";
    case "danger": return "border-red-200 bg-red-50 text-red-700 hover:bg-red-100";
    case "ghost": return "border-transparent text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100";
    case "secondary": default: return "border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50";
  }
}

export function Button({ children, className = "", disabled, type = "button", variant = "secondary", ...props }: ButtonProps): JSX.Element {
  return <button {...props} className={["inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-xl border px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50", variantClass(variant), className].join(" ")} disabled={disabled} type={type}>{children}</button>;
}


===== apps/web/src/shared/ui/Card.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/shared/ui/Card.tsx

import type { JSX, PropsWithChildren, ReactNode } from "react";

type CardProps = PropsWithChildren<{ title?: ReactNode; description?: ReactNode; actions?: ReactNode; className?: string; bodyClassName?: string }>;

export function Card({ title, description, actions, children, className = "", bodyClassName = "" }: CardProps): JSX.Element {
  const hasHeader = title || description || actions;
  return <section className={["rounded-2xl border bg-white shadow-sm", className].join(" ")}>{hasHeader ? <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-4 border-b bg-slate-50 px-5 py-4"><div>{title ? <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900">{title}</h2> : null}{description ? <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-600">{description}</p> : null}</div>{actions ? <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">{actions}</div> : null}</div> : null}<div className={["p-5", bodyClassName].join(" ")}>{children}</div></section>;
}


===== apps/web/src/shared/ui/DescriptionList.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/shared/ui/DescriptionList.tsx

import type { JSX, ReactNode } from "react";

export type DescriptionItem = { label: ReactNode; value: ReactNode };
type DescriptionListProps = { items: DescriptionItem[]; columns?: 1 | 2 | 3 };

function columnsClass(columns: 1 | 2 | 3): string {
  switch (columns) { case 3: return "md:grid-cols-3"; case 2: return "md:grid-cols-2"; case 1: default: return "md:grid-cols-1"; }
}

export function DescriptionList({ items, columns = 2 }: DescriptionListProps): JSX.Element {
  return <dl className={["grid gap-4 text-sm", columnsClass(columns)].join(" ")}>{items.map((item, index) => <div key={index} className="grid gap-1"><dt className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">{item.label}</dt><dd className="text-slate-900">{item.value}</dd></div>)}</dl>;
}


===== apps/web/src/shared/ui/EmptyState.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/shared/ui/EmptyState.tsx

import type { JSX, ReactNode } from "react";

type Props = {
  title: string;
  description?: string;
  action?: ReactNode;
};

export function EmptyState({ title, description, action }: Props): JSX.Element {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border bg-white p-8 text-center">
      <div className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">{title}</div>

      {description ? (
        <p className="mx-auto mt-2 max-w-xl text-sm text-slate-600">
          {description}
        </p>
      ) : null}

      {action ? <div className="mt-4">{action}</div> : null}
    </div>
  );
}

}

===== apps/web/src/shared/ui/LinkButton.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/shared/ui/LinkButton.tsx

import type { JSX, PropsWithChildren } from "react";
import { Link, type LinkProps } from "react-router-dom";

type LinkButtonVariant = "primary" | "secondary" | "danger" | "ghost";
type LinkButtonProps = PropsWithChildren<LinkProps & { variant?: LinkButtonVariant }>;

function variantClass(variant: LinkButtonVariant): string {
  switch (variant) {
    case "primary": return "border-transparent bg-slate-900 text-white hover:bg-slate-800";
    case "danger": return "border-red-200 bg-red-50 text-red-700 hover:bg-red-100";
    case "ghost": return "border-transparent text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100";
    case "secondary": default: return "border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50";
  }
}

export function LinkButton({ children, className = "", variant = "secondary", ...props }: LinkButtonProps): JSX.Element {
  return <Link {...props} className={["inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-xl border px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition", variantClass(variant), className].join(" ")}>{children}</Link>;
}

===== apps/web/src/shared/ui/StatusBadge.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/shared/ui/StatusBadge.tsx

import type { JSX } from "react";

type Props = {
  value: string;
};

function statusClass(value: string): string {
  const normalized = value.trim().toUpperCase();

  if (normalized === "CONCLUIDA" || normalized === "ATIVO") {
    return "border-green-200 bg-green-50 text-green-700";
  }

  if (normalized === "CANCELADA" || normalized === "ENCERRADO") {
    return "border-red-200 bg-red-50 text-red-700";
  }

  if (
    normalized === "ABERTA" ||
    normalized === "RASCUNHO" ||
    normalized === "EM_PROCESSAMENTO" ||
    normalized === "AGUARDANDO_PAGAMENTO"
  ) {
    return "border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 text-amber-700";
  }

  return "border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-slate-700";
}

export function StatusBadge({ value }: Props): JSX.Element {
  return (
    <span
      className={[
        "inline-flex rounded-full border px-2 py-1 text-xs font-medium",
        statusClass(value),
      ].join(" ")}
    >
      {value}
    </span>
  );
}


===== apps/web/src/shared/ui/SummaryCard.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/shared/ui/SummaryCard.tsx

import type { JSX, ReactNode } from "react";

type Props = {
  label: string;
  value: ReactNode;
  hint?: ReactNode;
};

export function SummaryCard({ label, value, hint }: Props): JSX.Element {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border bg-white p-4 shadow-sm">
      <div className="text-sm text-slate-500">{label}</div>
      <div className="mt-1 text-2xl font-semibold text-slate-900">{value}</div>
      {hint ? <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">{hint}</div> : null}
    </div>
  );
}


===== apps/web/src/shared/ui/Textarea.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/shared/ui/Textarea.tsx

import type { JSX, TextareaHTMLAttributes } from "react";

type TextareaProps = TextareaHTMLAttributes<HTMLTextAreaElement>;

export function Textarea({ className = "", ...props }: TextareaProps): JSX.Element {
  return <textarea {...props} className={["w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-900 outline-none transition placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-slate-400 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-slate-100 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-slate-50 disabled:text-slate-500", className].join(" ")} />;
}
